TL;DR: ReCan-GS：一种4D高斯泼溅框架，利用形变场雅可比得到的形变敏感度分数作为重建模糊性的经验代理，将场景分离为动态Core高斯与逐视角Distractor高斯，无需掩码或预训练分割器即可抑制瞬态遮挡、阴影与体积效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从随意拍摄的视频做动态重建时，需保留有效主体运动并抑制瞬态遮挡物、阴影和体积效应。现有方法要么以静态场景假设误抑制真实运动，要么联合建模全部观测，导致鬼影与几何不稳定。

Method: 提出ReCan-GS，基于4D高斯泼溅。从学习到的形变场导出基于雅可比的形变敏感度分数，作为重建模糊性的经验代理；该分数引导将高斯分离为动态Core高斯与逐视角Distractor高斯，或对敏感Core高斯进行事后剪枝。同一准则支持去除空间遮挡物、光度伪影及演化烟雾/雾气，且无需掩码、预训练分割器或介质特定监督。

Result: 在合成与真实动态场景以及合成烟雾/雾气场景上的实验表明，该方法在所评估的各类干扰情形下均有效。

Conclusion: 统一的形变敏感度准则可同时实现内容级分离与伪影剪枝，为随意视频下的鲁棒4D重建提供了无需额外监督的通用方案。

Abstract: Dynamic reconstruction from casual video must preserve valid subject motion while suppressing transient occluders, shadows, and volumetric effects. Existing methods either suppress valid motion through static-scene assumptions or model all observations jointly, producing ghosting and unstable geometry. We introduce ReCan-GS, a 4D Gaussian Splatting framework that separates coherent scene content from inconsistent observations. It derives a Jacobian-based deformation-sensitivity score from the learned deformation field, using it as an empirical proxy for reconstruction ambiguity. The score guides separation into dynamic Core Gaussians and per-view Distractor Gaussians, or post-hoc pruning of sensitive Core Gaussians. The same criterion supports removal of spatial occluders, photometric artifacts, and evolving smoke or fog without masks, pretrained segmenters, or medium-specific supervision. Experiments on synthetic and real dynamic scenes and synthetic smoke/fog scenes demonstrate its usefulness across the evaluated distractor regimes.

</details>


### [102] [FlowIR: Depth-driven Rectified Flow for Resolving Thermal Ambiguity in Infrared Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16269)
*Xingyue Zhu,Qinyun Mei,Zhexi Min,Yang Zou,Xingyuan Li,Zhiying Jiang,Jinyuan Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FlowIR是一个深度驱动的整流流框架，通过注入3D几何线索解决低分辨率红外图像中的热源歧义问题，实现少步数、高保真的红外超分辨率重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低分辨率红外图像中多个热辐射体常坍缩为模糊、重叠的响应，难以区分不同热源。现有超分辨率方法依赖模糊的2D证据虚构结构，导致热漂移、边界合并以及在红外退化下恢复不可靠。

Method: 提出FlowIR：基于轻量级整流流（rectified flow）骨干；采用伪3D深度提升与深度引导3D卷积解耦重叠前景目标；使用簇级互注意力（cluster-wise inter-attention）抑制跨热源特征污染；引入热-几何一致性损失（Thermal-Geometry Consistency Loss）在精化阶段强制几何对齐。

Result: 仅需少量采样步数即可恢复红外图像，生成边界清晰分离、抗退化且几何忠实的高分辨率结果。

Conclusion: 通过将3D几何先验注入生成过程并结合整流流高效采样，FlowIR有效缓解红外超分辨率中的热歧义问题，提升分离性与几何一致性。

Abstract: Distinguishing different heat sources in low-resolution infrared (IR) images is inherently difficult, as multiple thermal emitters often collapse into ambiguous and overlapping responses. Existing super-resolution methods hallucinate structures from such ambiguous 2D evidence, leading to thermal drift, boundary merging, and unreliable restoration under infrared degradations. We propose FlowIR, a depth-driven rectified flow framework that resolves thermal ambiguity by injecting 3D geometric cues into the generation process. Pseudo-3D depth lifting and depth-guided 3D convolutions disentangle overlapping foreground objects, while cluster-wise inter-attention suppresses cross-source feature contamination. A Thermal-Geometry Consistency Loss further enforces geometric alignment during refinement. Built upon a lightweight rectified flow backbone, FlowIR restores IR images in only a few steps, producing sharply separated, degradation-robust, and geometrically faithful high-resolution results.

</details>


### [103] [DEDCA: Test-Time Adaptation for Generalized AI-Generated Image Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16307)
*Hao Tan,Qimin Zhang,Lihuang Fang,Kebing Jin,Jinghui Qin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DEDCA测试时自适应框架，通过CLIP语义证据与低层痕迹证据的双证据融合及一致性/分歧约束，在无标签条件下适应未见生成器，GenImage上平均准确率92.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实场景中测试样本来自未见生成器，偏离训练分布；现有检测器依赖单一类型证据（语义表征或低层生成痕迹），线索失效时性能骤降；且适配新生成器通常需要目标标签或重训练，代价高且不实用。

Method: 构建双证据检测器：CLIP语义证据 + 由shuffled entropy与残差统计提取的痕迹证据；引入可靠性感知门控进行样本级自适应融合；以两路证据间的分歧作为无标签自适应信号，在不依赖目标标签、不盲目信任单一预测流的前提下调整检测器以适应目标域偏移。

Result: 在GenImage基准上优于现有SOTA AI生成图像检测器与测试时自适应方法，平均准确率达92.0%。

Conclusion: 双证据互补结合分歧驱动的无监督测试时适应可有效提升跨生成器泛化能力，为实际部署中的AI生成图像检测提供可行方案。

Abstract: AI-generated image detectors face significant challenges when deployed in real-world environments, particularly when test samples are produced by unseen generators that deviate from the source training distribution. Existing detectors often rely on a single type of evidence, such as semantic representations or low-level generation traces, making them vulnerable when the corresponding cue becomes unreliable. Moreover, adapting detectors to newly emerging generators usually requires target labels or retraining, which is costly and impractical. To address these challenges, we propose Dual-Evidence Disagreement-Constrained Adaptation (DEDCA), a test-time adaptation framework for generalized AI-generated image detection. DEDCA constructs a dual-evidence detector by combining CLIP-based semantic evidence with trace evidence extracted from shuffled entropy and residual statistics, and introduces a reliability-aware gate to perform sample-specific adaptive fusion. Our key idea is to exploit the disagreement between the two evidence streams as an unlabeled adaptation signal, enabling the detector to adjust to target-domain shifts without relying on target labels or blindly trusting a single prediction stream. Experiments show that DEDCA achieves superior performance on the GenImage benchmark by outperforming state-of-the-art AI-generated image detectors and test-time adaptation methods with 92.0\% average accuracy.

</details>


### [104] [Selective Generative Restoration for Video Deraining via Uncertainty-Aware Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16310)
*Yi'ang Chen,Han Li,Laibin Chang,Hui Liu,Kui Jiang,Junjun Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出选择性生成式视频去雨范式SGVD：利用双单步扩散预测的随机差异估计区域级修复不确定性，自适应决定何处保持观测保真、何处激活生成先验，并以锚点引导轨迹一致性抑制时序漂移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 雨退化具有空间异质性：可靠区域需忠实重建，严重遮挡区域内容模糊需更强先验推断。现有确定性范式会产生过度平滑，而全局生成式修复会引入不必要幻觉，二者均不理想。

Method: 基于"选择性生成式修复"原则构建不确定性感知扩散框架SGVD：1）以双单步扩散预测间的随机多样性估计区域级修复不确定性，据此决定保真保留与生成推断的空间分配；2）提出锚点引导的轨迹一致性机制，正则化跨帧随机扩散演化，减少时序生成漂移；3）采用单步扩散推理以兼顾效率。

Result: 大量实验表明，SGVD在修复性能、感知质量与时间一致性上均优于当前最先进的视频去雨方法，同时保持实际可用的推理效率。

Conclusion: 将生成先验的激活与修复不确定性显式耦合，可在避免全局幻觉的同时缓解确定性方法的过度平滑问题；单步扩散配合跨帧轨迹一致性，为视频去雨提供了兼顾保真度、感知质量与时序稳定性的可行方案。

Abstract: Video deraining is challenging due to rain streaks, occlusions, and atmospheric effects. Existing methods mainly follow a deterministic restoration paradigm, assuming that clean scenes can be uniquely recovered from rainy observations. However, rain degradation is spatially heterogeneous. Reliable regions require faithful reconstruction, whereas severely occluded regions contain ambiguous content that require stronger prior-driven inference. Therefore, conventional deterministic restoration often produces over-smoothed results, while global generative restoration may introduce unnecessary hallucinations. We introduce a Selective Generative Restoration paradigm for video deraining, where generative priors are adaptively activated according to restoration uncertainty. Based on this principle, we propose Selective Generative Video Deraining (SGVD), an uncertainty-aware diffusion framework that enables adaptive fidelity preservation and generative recovery. Specifically, SGVD exploits stochastic diversity between dual one-step diffusion predictions to estimate region-level restoration uncertainty, which determines where observation fidelity should be preserved and where generative inference should be activated. Furthermore, an anchor-guided trajectory consistency mechanism is proposed to regularize stochastic diffusion evolution across frames, reducing temporal generation drift and improving video coherence. To achieve practical efficiency, SGVD adopts one-step diffusion inference while maintaining uncertainty-aware generative capability. Extensive experiments demonstrate that SGVD achieves superior restoration performance, perceptual quality, and temporal consistency compared with state-of-the-art video deraining methods.

</details>


### [105] [Hand in Silhouette: Synthetic-to-Real Multi-View Hand Reconstruction via Binary Masks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16339)
*Leiyao Cui,Lecheng Ruan,Zihang Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SilHand：以二值手部掩码为观测接口的标定多视图手部重建框架，借助大规模合成数据缩小虚实域差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有标定多视图手部重建依赖真实采集数据，成本高、手部几何多样性受限且标注不完美；合成数据可扩展且监督精确，但存在显著的合成—真实外观域差距，阻碍其应用。

Method: 提出SilHand框架，输入为低成本获取（自动或手动）的二值手部掩码以缩小观测域差距；不使用RGB特征做三维提升，而是将度量尺度下的三维假设投影到各视图，检索并融合掩码射线证据完成重建。该掩码中心式表述支持在含46.8k抓取姿态与60k自由手样本的大规模合成数据集上训练。

Result: 在五个真实基准上，使用参考手部渲染掩码时相对重建精度由49.2%提升至63.4%；重建精度随合成训练集规模增长而一致提升；掩码损坏情形下误差最多增加1.63 mm，鲁棒性良好。

Conclusion: 二值掩码是有效的观测接口，可支撑从合成几何数据学习手部重建并直接迁移至真实手部几何；掩码中心式设计使广覆盖几何训练在实践上可行。

Abstract: Calibrated multi-view hand reconstruction has advanced rapidly, yet the effectiveness of existing methods depends strongly on real captured data, which is costly to acquire, limited in hand-geometry diversity, and often affected by imperfect annotations. Synthetic data offers a scalable alternative with accurate supervision, but a substantial synthetic-to-real appearance gap hinders its use. To address this, we introduce Hand in Silhouette (SilHand), a calibrated multi-view hand reconstruction framework that operates on binary hand masks, obtainable at low cost either automatically or manually, to reduce the synthetic-to-real observation gap. Rather than relying on RGB features for 3D lifting, SilHand projects metric 3D hypotheses into each view to query and fuse mask-ray evidence for hand reconstruction. This mask-centric formulation enables training on a large-scale synthetic dataset comprising 46.8k grasping poses and 60k free-hand samples, providing broad coverage of hand geometry while directly transferring to real captured hand geometry. Across five real-world benchmarks, SilHand improves relative reconstruction accuracy from 49.2% to 63.4% using masks rendered from reference hands. Moreover, reconstruction accuracy improves consistently as the synthetic training set grows, indicating that broader geometric coverage translates into better real-world performance. While achieving such diversity is difficult with real captured data, it becomes practical under our mask-centric formulation. Finally, SilHand remains robust under mask corruption, with reconstruction error increasing by at most 1.63 mm. Together, these results support binary masks as an effective interface for learning hand reconstruction from synthetic geometry.

</details>


### [106] [ReliaGuard: Reconstruction-Aware Watermarking for Source Attribution and Blind Edit Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16368)
*Hailong Wang,Wuzheng Tan,Zixin Zhang,Xujun Fang,Fengjuan Gong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ReliaGuard：一种重建感知水印框架，可在背景重建与后续图像处理下同时实现来源归属与盲编辑定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式图像编辑带来溯源与编辑验证难题；现有水印虽兼顾版权保护与篡改定位，但背景重建会削弱空间证据，即使源标识仍可恢复。

Method: 写入端在图像失真与来源保持约束下，联合优化受保护图像及其VAE重建中的密钥化特征响应；读取端仅凭接收图像与密钥，通过残差证据精化融合水印响应与局部图像上下文，并借助原生分辨率引导的多尺度读取预测编辑掩码；训练使用完整编辑输出、局部拼接样本及二者的处理后版本。

Result: 在覆盖三个图像域的300张源图、900个编辑案例独立测试集上，按完整输出1%背景误报率标定阈值，完整输出与局部拼接输出的编辑区域IoU分别为85.61%和94.90%（完整输出超最强基线45.02个百分点），标识比特准确率均达100%；在七类图像处理下，两种条件定位分数为81.01%和86.49%，比特准确率为92.76%和92.79%。

Conclusion: 结果证明该方法在背景重建及后续图像处理条件下仍能可靠恢复来源并实现盲编辑定位。

Abstract: Generative image editing facilitates content creation but complicates source attribution and edit verification. Existing watermarking methods combine copyright protection with tamper localization, yet background reconstruction can weaken spatial evidence even when source identifiers remain recoverable. We propose ReliaGuard, a reconstruction-aware watermarking framework for source attribution and blind edit localization. The writer optimizes keyed feature responses in both the protected image and its variational autoencoder reconstruction under image-distortion and source-preservation constraints. Using only the received image and a secret key, the reader combines watermark responses with local image context through residual evidence refinement and predicts an edit mask through native-resolution-guided multiscale reading. It is trained on complete editing outputs, locally spliced counterparts, and processed versions of both. On an independent test set of 300 source images and 900 editing cases from three image domains, our method achieves edit-region intersection-over-union scores of 85.61% for complete outputs and 94.90% for locally spliced outputs at a threshold calibrated to a nominal 1% background false-positive rate on complete outputs. The complete-output result exceeds the strongest baseline by 45.02 percentage points, while decoded identifier-bit accuracy reaches 100% in both conditions. Across seven families of image processing, the corresponding localization scores are 81.01% and 86.49%, with identifier-bit accuracies of 92.76% and 92.79%, respectively. These results support source recovery and blind edit localization under background reconstruction and subsequent image processing.

</details>


### [107] [Description-Guided CLIP Adaptation with Local Evidence Calibration for Few-Shot Plant Disease Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16373)
*Zhenkun Ye,Qian Zhou,Hua Zou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出描述引导的两阶段CLIP适配方法，用症状/对比描述增强类语义、以局部patch证据校准图文匹配，并在少样本植物病害识别与跨域泛化上取得最佳表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 少样本条件下标注样本稀少，难以覆盖诊断性症状与类内变异；现有CLIP类方法依赖粗粒度类语义与全局视觉表示，忽略病害特异性诊断信息与细粒度局部线索；过度少样本适配还会导致过拟合，削弱大规模预训练的泛化能力。

Method: 两阶段描述引导的CLIP适配。语义端：引入症状描述与对比描述，丰富类语义并刻画相似病害间的判别线索。视觉端：由增强语义引导聚合类相关patch证据，以细粒度线索校准全局图文匹配。适配策略：第一阶段以轻量参数更新视觉-语言编码器，第二阶段冻结编码器并训练线性分类器。推理：域内用分类器预测，域外借助适配后文本编码器的类原型预测，保留视觉-语言迁移性。实验采用两种骨干网络。

Result: 在PlantVillage、Cassava、Pear三个数据集上，多数少样本设置取得最高平均准确率；ViT-L/14、16-shot下分别达93.16%、79.30%、96.56%。在PlantDoc的21个共享类对齐跨域评测中达56.96%，优于所有对比方法。

Conclusion: 该方法有效提升细粒度少样本植物病害识别精度，并在跨域场景保持良好泛化，说明描述引导语义增强与局部证据校准结合、配合轻量两阶段适配可兼顾适配效果与预训练迁移能力。

Abstract: Accurate plant disease recognition is essential for intelligent agriculture, but few-shot settings provide limited labeled samples that may not capture diagnostic symptoms and intra-class variations. Pretrained vision-language models (VLMs), such as CLIP, offer transferable visual-semantic knowledge. However, existing CLIP-based methods often rely on coarse class semantics and global visual representations, overlooking disease-specific diagnostic information and fine-grained local cues. Excessive few-shot adaptation may also cause over-specialization and weaken the generalization ability of large-scale pre-training. We propose a description-guided, two-stage CLIP adaptation method to address these issues. Symptom and comparative descriptions enrich class semantics with typical visual symptoms and discriminative cues between similar diseases. The enhanced semantics guide the aggregation of class-relevant patch evidence to calibrate global image-text matching with fine-grained cues. For adaptation, we first update the vision-language encoders with lightweight parameters, then freeze them and train a linear classifier. At inference, the classifier is used for in-domain prediction, while class prototypes from the adapted text encoder support cross-domain prediction and retain vision-language transferability. Experiments with two backbones show that our method achieves the highest mean accuracy in most few-shot settings on PlantVillage, Cassava, and Pear, reaching 93.16%, 79.30%, and 96.56%, respectively, with ViT-L/14 under 16-shot. On class-aligned cross-domain evaluation across 21 shared PlantDoc classes, it achieves 56.96% accuracy, outperforming all compared methods. These results demonstrate its effectiveness in fine-grained few-shot plant disease recognition and cross-domain generalization.

</details>


### [108] [RADIANT: Redundancy-Aware Information Allocation for Universal Multi-Modal Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16382)
*Shenghai Wu,Yue Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出RADIANT框架，将多模态融合重构为"冗余抑制-独有信息保留-任务条件协同"三角色的信息分配问题。以矩阵指数投影降低共享/独有流间干扰，以硬正更新任务记忆选择激活的协同交互。在六个异构融合任务上一致提升，FLIR检测mAP@0.5由91.2%提升至96.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者认为通用多模态融合的失败根源不在模态对齐不足，而在融合过程的信息分配不当：冗余证据被反复放大，模态独有证据在对齐中被过度平滑，任务相关的协同证据激活不足。因此将融合重新表述为信息分配问题。

Method: RADIANT（Redundancy-Aware Dynamic Information Allocation Network）为任务通用框架，围绕三种互补角色组织融合：抑制跨模态冗余重叠、保留模态独有证据、通过跨模态交互构造任务条件协同。实现上采用稳定的矩阵指数（matrix-exponential）投影减小共享流与模态独有流之间的干扰；并引入带硬正更新（hard positive updates）的任务记忆，用于选择当前融合状态应激活的协同交互。

Result: 在六个异构融合任务上相对对比方法取得一致提升。FLIR数据集上，下游检测mAP@0.5由GIF的91.2%提升至96.8%，提升5.6个百分点（相对6.1%）。同时表现出较强的跨数据集直接迁移能力，且在冻结全部网络权重时仅通过推理期任务记忆自适应即可获得进一步增益。消融与敏感性研究支持该分配机制的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 多模态融合的关键瓶颈在于信息分配而非对齐，显式区分冗余、独有与协同三类证据并动态调度，可带来跨任务一致的性能与迁移收益；任务记忆支持的免训练推理期自适应是其额外优势。

Abstract: Universal multi-modal fusion often fails not because modalities are insufficiently aligned, but because the fusion process allocates information poorly: redundant evidence is repeatedly amplified, modality-unique evidence is over-smoothed during alignment, and task-dependent synergistic evidence is insufficiently activated. We present RADIANT (Redundancy-Aware Dynamic Information Allocation Network), a task-general framework that organizes fusion around three complementary roles: suppressing redundant cross-modal overlap, preserving modality-unique evidence, and composing task-conditioned synergy through cross-modal interaction. Specifically, stable matrix-exponential projections are used to reduce interference among shared and modality-unique streams, while task memory with hard positive updates selects which synergistic interactions should be activated for the current fusion regime. Extensive experiments across six heterogeneous fusion tasks show consistent improvements over the compared methods. On FLIR, RADIANT improves downstream detection mAP@0.5 from 91.2% to 96.8%, a gain of 5.6 percentage points (6.1% relative) over GIF. RADIANT also exhibits strong direct cross-dataset transfer, with further gains from inference-time task-memory adaptation while keeping all network weights frozen. Additional ablations and sensitivity studies support the robustness of the allocation mechanism.

</details>


### [109] [TransXplainRRG: A Radiologist-Evaluated LVLM for Chest Radiograph Report Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16393)
*Chayan Mondal,Duc-Son Pham,Tele Tan,Ashu Gupta,Vincent T. Y. Tiong,Shreya Ghosh,Tom Gedeon*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出TransXplainRRG框架，融合Swin Transformer视觉编码器、含病史的文本编码器与LLM，实现兼顾性能与可解释性的X射线报告生成，并在MIMIC-CXR与IU X-Ray上取得有竞争力的GREEN指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LVLM在X射线报告生成中忽视临床上下文、未融入工作流、缺乏可解释性，导致临床可解释性与可靠性存疑而难以落地应用。本文同时针对性能与专家可解释性两大挑战。

Method: 采用现成Swin Transformer作视觉编码器，结合融入患者病史的Transformer文本编码器生成报告；提出专家引导的“由内而外（inside-out）”策略，仅抽取异常征象用于报告精修；整合Transformer视觉/文本编码器与LLM。在MIMIC-CXR训练测试，并在未见过的IU X-Ray上验证泛化性。

Result: GREEN临床一致性指标在MIMIC-CXR为0.327、IU X-Ray为0.605，均高于同评测设置下的MAIRA-2；标准指标上多数持平或超越SOTA。另提出基于放射科医生视角的定性评价指标，并对100份生成报告开展初步放射科医生评估。

Conclusion: 定量与定性分析表明TransXplainRRG在可靠且可解释的放射报告生成方面具有潜力，弥合了高性能自动化与临床可解释性之间的差距。

Abstract: Recent advancements in Large Vision-Language Models (LVLM) enhance X-ray report generation, but often neglect clinical context, overlook workflow integration, and lack explainability, which hinders clinical adoption due to interpretability and reliability concerns. This paper addresses the dual challenges of performance and expert explainability in automated X-ray report generation for clinical AI applications. Our proposed approach, TransXplainRRG, utilises an off-the-shelf Swin Transformer model along with a transformer-based text encoder that incorporates patient medical history to generate a radiology report. Further, we explore an expert-guided `inside-out' approach and extract only abnormal findings for radiology report refinement. Thus, this study bridges the gap between high-performance automation and interpretability critical for clinical practice by combining state-of-the-art transformer-based vision encoders, text encoders, and LLMs. TransXplainRRG is trained and tested on the large-scale MIMIC-CXR dataset and further evaluated on the unseen IU X-Ray dataset to demonstrate its generalizability and robustness. We employed the GREEN metric as a complementary measure of clinical consistency, achieving scores of 0.327 on MIMIC-CXR and 0.605 on IU X-Ray, both higher than those achieved by MAIRA-2 under the same evaluation setting. It also performed competitively across standard evaluation metrics, frequently matching or surpassing state-of-the-art models. Additionally, we introduce qualitative evaluation metrics developed from radiologists' viewpoints and conduct a preliminary radiologist evaluation on 100 generated reports to assess their clinical relevance. Quantitative and qualitative analyses further demonstrate the potential of our TransXplainRRG framework for reliable and explainable radiology report generation.

</details>


### [110] [FuGIBench: Evaluating Reliable Preparation in Streaming Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16400)
*Changheng Cai,Zhichao Huang,Song Ren,Zhichen Zhao,Hongsen Liu,Chaoyang Zheng,Wenxi Wu,Yuan Fang,Wei Wang,Yang Wang,Yuchen Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出流式视频基准 FuGIBench，在"早期证据—响应到期"区间内评测模型在等待、可逆准备、响应三类决策间的选择，含19,893 条合成工具协助视频（十项工具任务，交接/日常活动/需求取消三条轨道），主分数奖励持续准备、对齐响应区间并惩罚应等待时的多余动作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频预判与交互响应基准基本未测量"早期线索出现"到"实际响应到期"这段准备区间，而助手恰需在此期间提前准备、维持准备、或在需求消失时撤回准备。缺少该区间度量，无法刻画准备行为的时机与撤回能力。

Method: 构建 FuGIBench：给定因果视频前缀与任务约定，模型在等待、可逆准备、响应之间决策；数据结构含 19,893 条合成工具协助视频、十类工具任务、三条轨道（交接、日常活动、需求取消）。设计主评分：奖励持续的准备状态，纳入响应区间对齐，并在参考标注为等待时惩罚模型产生活动。在冻结的 1,000 个片段上运行参考系统，并做取消情形分析。

Result: 在 1,000 个冻结片段上，准备提前量更长的参考系统响应对齐更差；配对场景中相近的交接提前量却对应日常工作中不同水平的活动量；取消分析进一步揭示需求消失时的撤回行为差异。

Conclusion: 评估"准备"必须跟踪决策随需求演进（发展、可执行、消失）的动态过程；仅看终点响应或单点预判会掩盖提前量与撤回能力的差异，FuGIBench 提供了可量化的区间化评测框架。

Abstract: An assistant watching a worker can prepare for an emerging need before the worker is ready to receive help.As the need evolves, the assistant must maintain preparation until a response is due, or withdraw it if the need disappears. Video anticipation and interactive response benchmarks leave this interval between early evidence and response largely unmeasured. We introduce FuGIBench (Future-Grounded Interaction), a streaming-video benchmark in which a model chooses among waiting, reversible preparation, and response given a causal video prefix and a task contract. The benchmark contains 19,893 synthetic tool-assistance videos across ten tool tasks, in handover, ordinary-activity, and cancelled-need tracks. One primary score credits sustained preparation, incorporates response-interval alignment, and penalizes activity when the reference prescribes waiting. On a frozen set of 1,000 episodes, a reference system with a longer preparation lead has poorer response alignment, while similar handover leads accompany different levels of activity during ordinary work in matched scenarios. Together with cancellation analyses, these results show why evaluating preparation requires tracking decisions as needs develop, become actionable, or disappear.

</details>


### [111] [Teach with Blinks: Breaking Linguistic Momentum in On-Policy Distillation for Fine-Grained Multimodal Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16402)
*Tangzheng Lian,Hang Zhang,Guanyu Hu,Renjiu Hu,Zhensong Zhang,Jiankang Deng,Jifei Song*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BlinkOPD 提出“眨眼”式师生蒸馏：教师对每个token分别在有/无前缀条件下各读一次，利用前缀差异强化纠正信号，缓解on-policy蒸馏中的语言惯性，仅用500张训练图即在六个细粒度基准上达到SOTA，并在十个留出基准上零样本提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大模型的细粒度感知通常依赖推理期放大（zooming），代价高昂；on-policy distillation（OPD）可将该能力摊销为单次前向。但朴素OPD存在“语言惯性”：教师会顺着学生错误的已生成前缀继续推理，导致纠正性监督被削弱，教师输出与真值偏离。

Method: 提出 BlinkOPD。教师对每个token“眨眼”两次：一次睁眼（读取与学生相同的完整前缀），一次闭眼（不依赖该前缀），通过两视图差异构造与上下文无关的监督信号；并引入 first-glance gain，在rollout与教师判断冲突时进一步放大纠正力度，从而从每条on-policy轨迹中提取更有效的监督。

Result: 仅用500张训练图像（比现有OPD方法少12倍数据）即在六个细粒度感知基准上取得SOTA平均性能；在十个留出基准（通用视觉感知、视觉幻觉、多模态推理）上展现出显著零样本提升。

Conclusion: 通过解耦前缀依赖的教师信号，BlinkOPD有效缓解语言惯性、提升单条轨迹的监督效率，实现数据高效且可泛化的多模态后训练，并开源代码。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) has emerged as an effective post-training approach to improving fine-grained perception in multimodal large language models by amortizing costly inference-time zooming operations into a single-pass recipe. However, vanilla OPD is inherently susceptible to linguistic momentum: the teacher can follow the student's erroneous prefixes, thus weakening corrective supervision. To address this, we propose Blink On-Policy Distillation (BlinkOPD), in which the teacher "blinks" at every token: it reads the same prefix once with its eyes open and once with its eyes closed. A first-glance gain further strengthens the correction on rollouts that conflict with the teacher. By extracting more effective supervision from each on-policy trajectory, BlinkOPD achieves state-of-the-art average performance across six fine-grained benchmarks using only 500 training images (12x less data) than existing OPD methods. Beyond fine-grained perception, BlinkOPD also demonstrates strong zero-shot improvements across ten held-out benchmarks spanning general visual perception, visual hallucination, and multimodal reasoning abilities. Code: https://github.com/Supltz/BlinkOPD

</details>


### [112] [Gravity and Flight: Learning to Navigate Fine-Grained Remote Sensing Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16408)
*Yuheng Zong,Minghua Wang,Xin Zhao,Xiaobin Liu,Yongjian Dong,Chanho Eom*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Gravity and Flight (G&F)框架，在有限监督下实现遥感场景专业化：分离视觉与推理验证并耦合策略更新，由EoT-SFT与RTPO组成，港口专业化平均得分69.12，较骨干与监督初始化分别提升9.76和4.56分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型能力不断增强，但在复杂遥感场景中的推理仍偏粗粒度，且未能充分建立在任务相关的视觉证据之上；同时场景专业化面临标注监督有限的约束。

Method: (1) EoT-SFT（Evidence-of-Thought 监督微调）：通过描述、分析、有据结论三阶段训练细粒度观察与忠实推理；(2) RTPO（Relational Transfer Policy Optimization）：对场景匹配、含细粒度视觉差异的图像对在同一任务下独立采样响应；(3) 基于评分细则的双重视角验证——Gravity检查视觉保真度与描述覆盖度，Flight以描述为前提评估证据使用及配对判断间关系；(4) 两视角得分与任务正确性构成共享的配对级优势，耦合两个响应的策略更新。

Result: 使用1,008条示范与500个训练对进行港口专业化，在六个评测集上平均得分69.12，超过骨干模型9.76分、超过监督初始化4.56分；匹配对照实验显示优于单图RL与联合输入RL，且移除任一验证视角都会导致性能下降；迁移到城市场景专业化后，在三个评测子集上同样优于基座模型与监督初始化。

Conclusion: 将视觉保真验证与推理证据验证解耦、并以配对级共享优势耦合策略更新，是有限监督下遥感场景专业化的有效途径，且具备跨场景可迁移性。

Abstract: Vision-language models are increasingly capable, yet their reasoning in complex remote sensing scenes can remain coarse-grained and insufficiently grounded in task-relevant visual evidence. We propose Gravity and Flight (G&F), a framework for scene specialization with limited supervision that separates visual and reasoning verification while coupling policy updates. It combines Evidence-of-Thought supervised fine-tuning (EoT-SFT) and Relational Transfer Policy Optimization (RTPO). EoT-SFT teaches fine-grained observation and faithful reasoning through description, analysis, and supported conclusions. RTPO independently samples responses to scene-matched image pairs with fine-grained visual differences under a shared task. Its rubric-based verification combines Gravity, which checks visual fidelity and description coverage, and Flight, which takes descriptions as premises to assess evidence use and relations between paired judgments. Their scores and task correctness yield a shared pair-level advantage that couples policy updates from both responses. Using 1,008 demonstrations and 500 training pairs for harbor specialization, G&F achieves a mean score of 69.12 across six evaluation sets, exceeding the backbone and supervised initialization by 9.76 and 4.56 points. Matched controls show gains over single-image and joint-input RL, and removing either verification view reduces performance. Applying G&F to urban specialization also improves on the base model and supervised initialization across three evaluation subsets. Project details and updates are available at https://github.com/Z0ngL1ng/Gravity-and-Flight.

</details>


### [113] [RICapBench: A Fine-Grained Visual Understanding Benchmark Dataset for Vision-Language Models in Remote Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16413)
*Xue Wang,Yubin Xu,Kun Tan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出RICapBench数据集与基准，将遥感图像的实例定位与细粒度描述统一评估，并提出空间关系解析器与RelCap-F1、综合指标RICScore，发现实例定位是15个先进VLM的主要瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型在遥感图像分析中虽有进展，但细粒度理解仍困难：目标尺度变化大、实例间地理空间关系复杂、视觉细节有限，导致实例定位、可见属性识别与空间关系理解不精确，描述缺乏视觉依据。现有数据集与基准不足以在无目标特定文本线索条件下评估端到端实例定位与描述能力。

Method: 构建RICapBench，涵盖通用遥感、无人机遥感与密集小目标三类场景，包含245,466条实例级标注与498,005个空间关系三元组，每条标注将定向边界框与描述可见属性及与周边实例、环境要素空间关系的自然语言配对；提出遥感空间关系解析器与RelCap-F1指标，并与实例定位、属性描述指标组合成复合指标RICScore；评测15个开源与闭源VLM。

Result: 评测显示实例定位是这些模型的主要瓶颈；LocateAnything取得最高RICScore 31.68，但其空间关系描述得分低于其他经任务微调的开源VLM。

Conclusion: RICapBench为遥感细粒度实例定位与描述提供统一评估平台，揭示定位能力不足制约端到端表现，代码与数据集已公开。

Abstract: Advances in vision-language models (VLMs) have improved remote sensing image analysis and supported geospatial decision-making. However, fine-grained understanding of remote sensing imagery remains challenging for VLMs. Large variations in object scale, complex geospatial relations among instances, and limited visual detail hinder precise instance localization, visible attribute identification, and spatial relation understanding, potentially leading to descriptions that lack visual grounding. Existing datasets and benchmarks insufficiently assess end-to-end instance localization and captioning without target-specific textual cues. To support this evaluation, we introduce RICapBench, a dataset and benchmark that unifies instance localization and fine-grained captioning in remote sensing. RICapBench contains 245,466 instance-level annotations and 498,005 spatial relation tuples spanning general remote sensing, UAV remote sensing, and dense small-object scenarios. Each annotation pairs an object's oriented bounding box with a natural language description of its visible attributes and spatial relations to surrounding instances and environmental elements. To evaluate the accuracy and completeness of spatial relation captioning, we propose a remote sensing spatial relation parser and the RelCap-F1 metric. We combine RelCap-F1 with instance localization and attribute captioning metrics into a composite metric, RICScore. Our evaluation of 15 advanced open-source and closed-source VLMs identifies instance localization as a major bottleneck for these models. LocateAnything achieves the highest RICScore of 31.68, but scores lower on spatial relation captioning than other task-fine-tuned open-source VLMs. Code and dataset are available at https://github.com/Xldln/RICapBench.

</details>


### [114] [VBot Exo2Ego Policy: Lightweight Data Collection and Exocentric-to-Egocentric Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16417)
*Tong Rao,Zhihao Zhang,Yuntian Wang,Siyu Zhou,Hailong Qin,Yinan Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 VBot Exo2Ego Policy，一个视频生成模型，将第三人称视频转换为指定操作者的第一人称观测，用于从丰富真实视频中扩展第一人称操作数据；配套轻量数据采集流程与移动端 App。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第一人称操作数据稀缺，而真实世界第三人称视频资源丰富；需要一种能可靠跨视角转换的方法来扩充第一人称操作数据。

Method: 1）设计轻量采集管线与移动 App，支持第一人称、第三人称及双视角同步采集，兼顾单视角易采集与配对 RGB+相机几何；2）基于预训练视频扩散 Transformer，引入相机几何引导，并用语义掩码与手部关键点做辅助监督以提升手-物交互保真度；3）训练时随机注入相机几何并逐步移除，实现推理时无需目标相机标定；4）结合源视频感知与推理、可选操作者参考、多镜头适配，以识别目标操作者并跨镜头切换跟踪操作。

Result: 模型可在无目标相机标定条件下由第三人称视频生成对应第一人称观测，改善手-物交互保真度，并支持多镜头与多主体场景下的操作者识别与跟踪。

Conclusion: 采集系统与生成模型共同构成基础框架，可用于跨任务、跨场景、跨参与者地扩展第一人称操作数据。

Abstract: We present VBot Exo2Ego Policy, a video generation model that turns third-person videos into first-person observations of a selected actor, offering a practical way to expand first-person operation data from abundant real-world footage. To support this goal, we introduce a lightweight data collection pipeline and mobile app that support first-person recording, third-person recording, and synchronized capture of both views. This setup combines accessible single-view collection with paired RGB and camera geometry for learning cross-view correspondence. Building on a pretrained video diffusion transformer, our model incorporates geometric guidance and auxiliary supervision from semantic masks and hand keypoints to facilitate optimization and improve the fidelity of hand-object interactions. Camera geometry is injected stochastically during training and gradually removed, allowing inference without target-camera calibration. To accommodate the diversity of Internet videos, we combine source-video perception and reasoning, optional actor references, and multi-shot adaptation to identify the intended actor and follow an operation across camera cuts. The collection system and generation model provide a foundation for expanding first-person operation data across tasks, scenes, and participants.

</details>


### [115] [Model Distribution-Aware Multimodal Dataset Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16418)
*Jiajun Shen,Yonglin Wu,Ruonan Yu,Xinchao Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MDA-DD，用不同训练阶段的模型动态队列提供监督，结合非对称双投影与非中心二阶矩目标，提升多模态数据集蒸馏的泛化性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态数据集蒸馏（MDD）的特征匹配方法使合成数据过拟合于优化时所见模型表征，难以泛化到未见模型状态。

Method: 构建跨训练阶段的动态交错模型队列作为监督来源；引入非对称双投影实现视觉与语言空间的互补对齐；采用非中心二阶矩目标以保留与均值相关的跨模态信息。

Result: 在Flickr30K与MS-COCO上于各合成数据预算下检索性能一致优于现有方法；仅用100对合成数据，Flickr30K的IR@1与TR@1较同预算最强基线分别提升12.9与18.2个百分点，并在六项检索指标上全面超越使用500对数据的所有对比方法。

Conclusion: 缓解模型特定性可显著增强多模态蒸馏数据的泛化能力，MDA-DD在小预算下即可匹敌或超越大预算基线。

Abstract: Multimodal dataset distillation (MDD) aims to reduce the cost of vision-language pre-training by synthesizing compact image-text datasets. Although recent feature-matching methods have improved distillation efficiency, synthetic data may become specialized to the model representations encountered during optimization, limiting generalization to unseen model states. We propose Model Distribution-Aware Multimodal Dataset Distillation (MDA-DD), which leverages supervision from a dynamic staggered queue of models at different training stages. We further introduce asymmetric dual projection for complementary alignment in visual and linguistic spaces and an uncentered second-order moment objective that retains mean-related cross-modal information. Experiments on Flickr30K and MS-COCO demonstrate consistent retrieval improvements over existing methods across synthetic-data budgets. With only 100 synthetic pairs, MDA-DD improves IR@1 and TR@1 on Flickr30K by 12.9 and 18.2 percentage points, respectively, over the strongest baseline at the same budget, outperforming all compared methods using 500 pairs on both datasets across all six retrieval metrics.

</details>


### [116] [PhysCapBench: A Cross-Domain Benchmark for Physically Grounded Video Captioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16424)
*Haofeng Wang,Pengcheng Wen,Ruiqi Li,Yuhao Zhang,Tiange Zhang,Boren Zheng,Xuanyi Liu,Siyu Tao,Danni Yu,Gan Lin,Haocheng Tang,Tianzizhe Guo,Mingyuan Su,Boyuan Chen,Zhongyuan Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PhysCapBench——首个跨第三人称场景、自我中心操作与手持夹爪演示三大域的统一"物理描述"评测基准，含1,500段视频的人工核验标注，用七个物理维度评估准确性与覆盖度，并验证其下游价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理AI需从视频/具身演示中学习可迁移的物理知识，但语义正确的标题可能遗漏定义过程本身的交互与状态转移，视频中的丰富物理经验并不能自动转化为充分的语言监督；因此缺少一把衡量标注质量、比较描述策略、指导训练数据精修的"尺子"。

Method: 构建PhysCapBench统一基准：覆盖第三人称场景、自我中心操作、手持夹爪演示三域，为1,500段视频序列提供人工核验标注，并通过七个物理维度评估描述的准确性与覆盖度；以下游视频重建与操作策略微调（含RoboTwin 2.0子任务上pi0.5）检验其实用性。

Result: 相对元数据描述，物理描述使第三人称视频重建LPIPS降低8.6–9.5%，自我中心视频降低26.9–30.9%；训练指令增强同时提升PhysCapBench分数与策略成功率，pi0.5在RoboTwin 2.0子任务上提升7.5个百分点。

Conclusion: PhysCapBench使语言监督中的物理内容可被检视与比较，为跨域的物理描述评测与改进提供统一基础。

Abstract: Physical AI aims to learn transferable physical knowledge from real-world experience to understand, predict, and act in the physical world. Internet videos and embodied demonstrations provide complementary observations of motion, interaction, and state evolution. A foundational data challenge is to measure and preserve this physical information as training supervision. In video-language pretraining, captions express observed processes in language, yet a semantically correct description can omit the interactions and transitions that define those processes. Rich physical experience in video does not automatically translate into sufficient language supervision. This gap calls for a yardstick to assess annotation quality, compare captioning strategies, and guide training-data refinement. Motivated by our data construction for world-model pretraining, we introduce PhysCapBench, to our knowledge the first unified physical-caption benchmark spanning all three domains: third-person scenes, egocentric manipulation, and handheld-gripper demonstrations. It provides human-verified annotations for 1,500 video sequences and evaluates accuracy and coverage through seven physical dimensions. We examine downstream utility through video reconstruction and manipulation-policy fine-tuning. Physical captions reduce reconstruction LPIPS by 8.6-9.5% on third-person videos and 26.9-30.9% on egocentric videos relative to metadata captions. Training-instruction enrichment improves both PhysCapBench scores and policy success, including a 7.5-percentage-point gain for pi0.5 on a RoboTwin 2.0 subtask. PhysCapBench makes the physical content of language supervision inspectable and comparable, providing a cross-domain basis for its evaluation and improvement.

</details>


### [117] [DesignTaste: Benchmarking AI-Generator Visual Design Against Award-Wining Works](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16442)
*Zhou Chen,JiaXiang Li,DanYing Pan,Ye Li,TianRui Yang,EnBao Zhang,YaNan Xiao,LiXin Sun,Anqi Liu,Dong Zhao,JianYuan Li,YuanYuan Ding,Kai Kong,Xinwen Xu,HuiTang Du,Min Zhou,Zhiqiang Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出视觉设计基准 DesignTaste，以获奖设计作品为参照，用 403 条开放式设计简报评测 6 个商业文生图模型，结果显示仅 ArtArk 平均总分超过获奖作品，而全部模型在“创意原创性”维度均落后。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文生图基准多评估文本-图像对齐或整体偏好，但对“简报未限定外观时模型能否给出好设计方案”这一能力缺乏了解，因此需要一个以专业获奖设计为参照、检验设计问题求解能力的基准。

Method: 从获奖作品中及其公开背景信息提取 403 条设计简报（100 条海报任务，源自 100 Beste Plakate 2025；303 条任务，覆盖 ADC 2026 的 20 个类别）。6 个领先商业文生图模型对同一简报各生成一张图像；由单一模型裁判对模型输出与对应获奖作品，在五个评测维度上分别独立打分。

Result: 在两个数据集上，仅 ArtArk 的平均总体得分高于获奖作品；6 个模型在两个数据集上的“创意原创性”（Creative Originality）得分均更低。

Conclusion: 能生成完成度高、视觉悦目的图像并不等同于模型具备良好的设计解决方案能力；文生图模型在设计原创性上仍与专业设计存在明显差距。

Abstract: Text-to-image benchmarks commonly assess text-image alignment or overall preference. Whether models can produce good designs when a brief leaves the appearance open remains less well understood. We introduce DesignTaste, a visual design benchmark that compares generated images with award-winning works. From these works and their publicly available context, we derive 403 design briefs: 100 poster tasks from 100 Beste Plakate 2025 and 303 tasks spanning 20 categories from ADC 2026. Six leading commercial text-to-image models each generate an image from the same brief. A single model judge scores each output and the corresponding award-winning work independently on five evaluation dimensions. On each dataset, only ArtArk achieves a higher mean overall score than the award-winning works. All six models score lower in Creative Originality on both datasets. These results show that producing finished, visually appealing images does not imply that a model can develop good design solutions.

</details>


### [118] [HIFC-IQA: Train-Free Cross-Domain Image Quality Assessment via Dual-Process Cognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16451)
*Yu Li,Zhengran Shen,Puchao Zhou,Yukang Ding,Yachun Mi,Boyuan Yang,Xi Zhai,Shaohui Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出无需训练（train-free）的跨域无参考图像质量评价框架 HIFC，借鉴人类认知双过程理论，通过非对称感知分解分离语义内容与失真敏感表征，融合"记忆共识"与"直觉"两分支输出质量分数，在多个基准上取得UDA方法中最优平均SRCC/PLCC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合成失真与真实世界复杂退化之间存在域偏移，构成无参考图像质量评价（NR-IQA）的核心挑战。现有无监督域适应（UDA）方法依赖大量重训练开销，且迭代伪标签带来累积误差传播，限制了泛化能力。

Method: HIFC 为完全免训练的跨域IQA框架：(1) 采用非对称感知分解，将语义内容与失真敏感表征解耦；(2) 记忆共识分支——从合成参考图库中检索稳定的"模糊质量质心"（Fuzzy Quality Centroid）；(3) 直觉分支——通过零样本视觉-语言对齐捕捉美学极化信息；(4) 两分支协同融合得到最终质量分数。

Result: 在多个基准上达到SOTA，竞争性UDA方法中平均SRCC与PLCC最高；在合成到真实（synthetic-to-authentic）的跨域设定下相对对比方法取得显著性能领先，体现较强鲁棒性与泛化性。

Conclusion: 借鉴认知双过程理论的免训练跨域IQA范式可有效规避UDA的重训练与伪标签误差累积问题，为跨域质量评价提供高效、可泛化的新思路。

Abstract: The gap between controlled synthetic distortions and complex real-world degradations introduces a critical domain shift challenge for No-Reference Image Quality Assessment. Although Unsupervised Domain Adaptation (UDA) approaches attempt to address this issue, they remain constrained by substantial retraining overhead and cumulative error propagation inherent in iterative pseudo-labeling. Inspired by the dual-process theory of human cognition for remarkable generalization ability, we propose Hierarchical Intuitive and Fuzzy Consensus (HIFC), a completely train-free Cross-Domain IQA framework. HIFC employs asymmetric perceptual decomposition to disentangle semantic content from distortion-sensitive representations. The final quality score is fused through the synergy of two complementary branches: a memory consensus branch that retrieves a stable Fuzzy Quality Centroid from a synthetic reference gallery, and an intuitive branch that captures aesthetic polarization via zero-shot vision-language alignment. Extensive experiments show that HIFC achieves state-of-the-art performance across multiple benchmarks, attaining the highest average SRCC and PLCC among competing UDA methods. Most notably, in the challenging synthetic-to-authentic cross-domain setting, HIFC achieves a substantial performance margin over competing methods, demonstrating remarkable robustness and generalization.

</details>


### [119] [Can an LVLM Perceive Event Boundaries Like a Human?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16452)
*Hyolim Kang,Jungsoo Lee,Yeji Song,Whie Jung,Janghoon Cho,Juntae Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文研究LVLM是否能以类人方式感知视频事件边界，提出“发现-精炼”流程：先用无监督方法提出候选边界，再由LVLM进行接受/拒绝判断，并扩展到小时级第一人称视频，构建EgoBoundary基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LVLM对视频内容推理有效，但其事件边界感知是否与人类一致尚不明确；且现有GEBD多为短片段，小时级长时程第一人称视频中的边界检测面临语义尺度更广、全视频LVLM精炼不可行的挑战。

Method: 通过GEBD任务在不依赖预定义类别的前提下考察LVLM边界感知；在Kinetics-GEBD上验证“无监督发现候选+LVLM精炼”范式；对长视频采用由粗到细的时间发现与局部化LVLM接受/拒绝判断；并进行人类评测，比较LVLM与群体共识的一致性。

Result: LVLM直接从帧预测会漏检大量边界，但精炼无监督候选可达SOTA无监督GEBD性能；其与群体共识的一致性落在人类标注者间差异范围内，分歧集中于低共识样本；代表性监督与无监督方法在EgoBoundary上显著性能下降。

Conclusion: LVLM适合作为边界候选的判别器而非直接检测器，发现-精炼范式可有效迁移到长时程第一人称视频；EgoBoundary揭示了长时程事件边界检测仍是显著挑战。

Abstract: Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) reason effectively about video content, but whether they perceive its event boundaries in a manner consistent with humans remains unclear. We first investigate this through Generic Event Boundary Detection (GEBD), which identifies event boundaries naturally perceived by humans in continuous video, without relying on predefined event categories. On Kinetics-GEBD dataset, LVLMs miss many boundaries when predicting directly from frames, but effectively refine candidates proposed by an unsupervised discovery method, achieving state-of-the-art unsupervised GEBD performance. We extend this discovery-refinement approach to hour-long egocentric video, where boundary discovery requires a broader semantic scale and whole-video LVLM refinement becomes impractical. We address these challenges through coarse-to-fine temporal discovery and localized LVLM accept/reject judgments. Human evaluation shows that the LVLM's agreement with group consensus falls within the range observed among human annotators, with disagreements concentrated on low-consensus cases. Using this pipeline, we construct EgoBoundary, a benchmark for event boundary detection in hour-scale egocentric video. Representative supervised and unsupervised methods degrade substantially on EgoBoundary, highlighting the challenge of long-horizon event boundary detection.

</details>


### [120] [PiLLVD: A Real-World Low-Light Video Dataset for Dynamic Scenes Captured with Off-the-Shelf Cameras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16515)
*Yaser Abu Awwad,Paul L. Rosin,Oktay Karakuş*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: The paper introduces PiLLVD, a Raspberry Pi–based multi-camera paired low-light video dataset for motion-rich indoor/outdoor scenes, and benchmarks zero-shot low-light video enhancement methods on it.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Low-light enhancement research is dominated by still images; video enhancement progress is limited by the lack of real-world paired low-light/normal-light video datasets. Capturing such pairs requires spatial and temporal alignment while avoiding motion blur, exposure mismatch and correspondence errors in dynamic scenes.

Method: Build an accessible multi-camera acquisition system with off-the-shelf cameras on a Raspberry Pi to record spatially and temporally aligned paired low-light and normal-light video sequences of dynamic indoor and outdoor scenes, containing diverse human and object motion under varying illumination.

Result: PiLLVD provides paired, aligned sequences emphasizing motion-rich scenes where frame-to-frame stability and motion artefacts are critical; unlike existing datasets it is not image-based, static, synthetic, or restricted to simple motion. Zero-shot benchmarking of representative pre-trained methods shows non-uniform generalisation across sequences.

Conclusion: Existing low-light video enhancement methods do not generalise uniformly on PiLLVD, demonstrating the dataset's challenge and its value for benchmarking and for developing future algorithms.

Abstract: Low-light image and video enhancement has received increasing attention in recent years. However, most existing enhancement methods are developed and evaluated on still images, while progress in low-light video enhancement remains limited by the lack of real-world paired video datasets. Acquiring paired low-light and normal-light video sequences is challenging because it requires spatially and temporally aligned captures while avoiding motion blur, exposure mismatch, and correspondence errors in dynamic scenes. To address this gap, we introduce PiLLVD, the Pi-based Low-Light Video Dataset, a paired low-light video dataset captured using an accessible Raspberry Pi-based multi-camera system with off-the-shelf cameras. PiLLVD provides paired low-light and aligned normal-light reference sequences for dynamic indoor and outdoor scenes, including diverse human and object motion under varying illumination conditions. Unlike existing datasets that are often image-based, static, synthetic, or limited to simple motion patterns, PiLLVD focuses on motion-rich scenes where frame-to-frame stability and motion-related artefacts are important for evaluation. In addition to presenting the dataset and acquisition pipeline, we benchmark representative pre-trained low-light video enhancement methods on PiLLVD under a zero-shot setting. The results show that existing methods do not generalise uniformly across the proposed sequences, highlighting the challenges posed by the proposed dataset and its value for both benchmarking existing methods and supporting the development of future low-light video enhancement algorithms.

</details>


### [121] [UniSpark: Exploring Hybrid Attention for Multimodal Understanding and Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16517)
*Jiancheng Pan,Yuzheng Liu,Hengyuan Cao,Qiyuan Zhang,Yangyan Li,Biao Gong,Zheng Zhang,Ka Long Cheng,Xinyang Wang,Yujun Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UniSpark-9B 提出以混合注意力（全注意力 + GatedDeltaNet 线性注意力）为核心骨干的统一多模态模型，在 ~9B 参数规模下同时支持多模态理解与生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有统一多模态模型在处理理解（需精确 token 交互）与生成（需长上下文、紧凑状态传播）时，单一注意力机制难以兼顾效率与表达能力；同时交错图文生成中"噪声视觉目标"与"干净视觉上下文"的隔离问题缺乏系统性建模。论文旨在验证混合注意力能否作为统一理解与生成的有效骨干。

Method: 基于 Mixture-of-Transformer-Experts 骨干，每分支约 9B 参数；结合全注意力（显式 token 交互）与 GatedDeltaNet 线性注意力（紧凑上下文传播）。采用自回归文本预测与视觉 flow matching 联合训练，覆盖 VQA、文生图、指令引导编辑与交错图文预测。交错生成中通过分块协议隔离噪声视觉目标：全注意力层用注意力掩码、线性注意力层用递归状态保留规则，使干净视觉表示作为后续输出上下文。训练数据含 159.7M 生成样本、213.4M 理解样本、624.8K 交错序列，采用四阶段课程联合训练。

Result: MME-S 得分 2470.3，GenEval 0.83；图像合成分辨率扩展至 2K；定性示例展示交错图像生成、视频合成与视频编辑能力。

Conclusion: 混合注意力可作为统一多模态理解与生成的有效骨干，兼顾显式交互与高效上下文传播，并支持交错图文生成等复杂任务。

Abstract: We present UniSpark-9B, a unified multimodal model that investigates hybrid attention for visual understanding and generation. Built on a Mixture-of-Transformer-Experts backbone with approximately 9B parameters in each branch, UniSpark combines full attention for explicit token interactions with GatedDeltaNet linear attention for compact context propagation. Autoregressive text prediction and visual flow matching jointly support visual question answering, text-to-image generation, instruction-guided editing, and interleaved text-image prediction. In interleaved generation, noisy visual targets are isolated from subsequent predictions, while clean visual representations provide context for later outputs. A blockwise protocol enforces this distinction through attention masks in full-attention layers and recurrent-state retention rules in linear-attention layers. The selected training sources contain 159.7M generation samples, 213.4M understanding samples, and 624.8K interleaved sequences. A four-stage curriculum jointly trains multimodal understanding and visual generation, refining answer accuracy and instruction following while extending image synthesis to 2K. UniSpark scores 2470.3 on MME-S and 0.83 on GenEval. Qualitative examples further illustrate its capabilities in interleaved image generation, video synthesis, and video editing. Together, these results demonstrate that hybrid attention provides an effective backbone for unified multimodal understanding and generation.

</details>


### [122] [Context-Predictive Low-Rank Adaptation for Face Presentation Attack Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16534)
*Aminu Yusuf,Shakeel Muhammad Ibrahim,Sulaimon Oyeniyi Adebayo,Muhammad Sohail Ibrahim,Muhammad Shahid Jabbar,Shujaat Khan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CP-JEPA：结合CLIP的LoRA低秩微调与联合嵌入预测目标（JEPA）的人脸呈现攻击检测框架，学生预测掩码位置的完整图像教师特征，推理阶段仅需LoRA微调后的CLIP编码器与二分类器，在OCIM、CelebA-Spoof、SiW-Mv2上取得优秀跨库性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人脸呈现攻击检测需要能跨采集设备、受试者和攻击媒介区分物理欺骗线索的表征。大视觉编码器具有可迁移表征，但常规二值微调只监督最终判决，未显式约束局部人脸证据在部分观测下的表征方式。

Method: 在预训练CLIP图像编码器上做LoRA低秩微调，并引入联合嵌入预测目标：学生网络在掩码空间位置预测完整图像教师特征；同时保留未掩码分类、掩码分类与预测一致性三项损失以维持活体/欺骗任务关联；教师由EMA更新提供演化潜在目标。辅助模块仅用于训练。

Result: OCIM标准基准上3.74% mean HTER、98.73% mean AUC；单源评估12个源–目标组合平均HTER 11.36%。三源OCIM训练加入CelebA-Spoof后，mean HTER由3.45%降至2.35%，mean AUC由98.68%升至99.32%。SiW-Mv2未见攻击类别上mean HTER 3.36%。消融实验考察了潜在预测、掩码分类与预测一致性的贡献。

Conclusion: 上下文预测式适配可提升跨数据集PAD性能，但增益随训练源与评估设置而变化。

Abstract: Face presentation attack detection requires representations that distinguish physical spoofing cues across changes in acquisition devices, subjects, and attack media. Large visual encoders offer transferable representations, but conventional binary fine-tuning supervises their final decisions without explicitly constraining how local facial evidence is represented under partial observation. We present CP-JEPA, a context-predictive adaptation framework that combines low-rank tuning of a pretrained CLIP image encoder with a joint-embedding predictive objective. A student predicts complete-image teacher features at masked spatial locations, while unmasked classification, masked classification, and prediction consistency retain the connection to the live/spoof task. An exponential-moving-average teacher supplies evolving latent targets. The auxiliary components are used only during training; inference requires only the LoRA-adapted CLIP visual encoder and binary classifier. {We evaluate CP-JEPA under single-source and multi-source OCIM transfer, additional CelebA-Spoof training, transfer to CelebA-Spoof, two-source training, and unseen-attack evaluation on SiW-Mv2.} On the standard OCIM benchmark, CP-JEPA achieves 3.74\% mean HTER and 98.73\% mean AUC. {Single-source evaluation achieves 11.36\% mean HTER across twelve source--target combinations.} With CelebA-Spoof added to the three OCIM training sources, CP-JEPA reduces mean HTER from 3.45\% to 2.35\% and increases mean AUC from 98.68\% to 99.32\%. On SiW-Mv2, it achieves 3.36\% mean HTER across the held-out attack categories. Controlled loss ablations examine the contributions of latent prediction, masked classification, and prediction consistency. The results show that context-predictive adaptation can improve cross-dataset PAD performance, although the gains vary with the training sources and evaluation setting.

</details>


### [123] [Pl@ntNet machine-to-machine identification service with Pl@ntNet API: enabling automated biodiversity monitoring through large-scale plant identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16538)
*Benjamin Bourel,Jean-Christophe Lombardo,Alexis Joly,Mathias Chouet,Thomas Paillot,Antoine Affouard,Vanessa Hequet,Herv{é} Go{ë}au,Pierre Bonnet*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Pl@ntNet 提出机器对机器的 AI 植物识别 API 服务，覆盖全球 8.5 万物种，物种预测准确率达 0.89，处理速度最高比人工流程快 666 倍，自上线以来已处理超 8400 万次识别请求，并已支撑传粉互作重建、社交媒体分布分析、入侵植物检测和农业机器人除草等多种自动化生态工作流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 植物多样性监测需要在时空尺度上识别大量植物。现有 AI 识别系统虽精度高，但多依赖用户手动上传图像，难以适配相机、无人机、机器人及在线平台产生的大规模图像集合；因此亟需可嵌入自动化数据处理流程的植物识别服务。

Method: 作者开发了 Pl@ntNet 的机器对机器 AI 植物识别服务，通过应用程序接口（API）以编程方式访问其植物识别模型及配套的分类学处理流程，实现无需人工干预的自动化识别工作流，并给出可复现示例（处理 iNaturalist 昆虫照片上的植物）。

Result: 服务覆盖约 85,000 个全球物种；物种预测准确率 0.89；处理速度最高为人工流程的 666 倍；累计处理识别请求超 8400 万次（2025 年单年超 4000 万次）。应用案例包括：由昆虫图像重建植物—传粉者互作、基于社交媒体地理定位图像研究植物分布、路边图像检测入侵植物、农业机器人识别杂草。可复现示例中，普通笔记本约 30 分钟处理 2,424 张图像，生成 708 条植物—传粉者互作记录，花检测加权精度 0.98，属级植物识别准确率 0.88。

Conclusion: 该机器对机器物种识别服务已在大规模上实际运行并满足真实需求，为生物多样性监测开辟新范式：自动传感器与图像集合可直接对接 AI 识别服务。

Abstract: 1. Monitoring plant biodiversity requires identifying large numbers of plants across space and time. Although artificial intelligence (AI) can now identify plant species from images with high accuracy, most existing systems rely on users manually submitting images. As ecological monitoring increasingly generates large image collections from cameras, drones, robots and online platforms, there is a growing need for plant identification services that can be directly integrated into automated data-processing workflows. 2. Here, we present Pl@ntNet's machine-to-machine AI plant identification service. This service uses an application programming interface (API) to provide programmatic access to Pl@ntNet's plant recognition models and the associated taxonomic processing. This service enables the implementation of automated identification workflows without human intervention and can thus directly link large image collections and automated observation systems to an AI-based plant identification system. 3. We establish that this service, which covers 85,000 species from around the world, (1) has a species prediction accuracy of 0.89, (2) process images up to 666 times faster than workflows requiring human intervention, (3) and has processed over 84 million identification requests since its launch, including more than 40 million in 2025 alone. We demonstrate how it has already enabled the implementation of a new generation of ecological workflows: identifying plants from images of insects to reconstruct the plant-pollinator interactions, analysing geolocated images from social media to study plant distribution, detecting invasive plants using images taken at the roadside, and identifying weeds using agricultural robots. Finally, we illustrate its application using a reproducible example: identifying plants from insect photos on iNaturalist. In approximately 30 min on a standard laptop, 2,424 images were processed to generate a dataset of 708 plant-pollinator interactions, with a weighted precision of 0.98 for flower detection and an accuracy of 0.88 for genus plant identification. 4. In conclusion, this machine-to-machine species identification is already operating on a considerable scale and meets a genuine need. All of this paves the way for a new paradigm in biodiversity monitoring, in which automated sensors and image collections can be directly connected to AI-based identification services.

</details>


### [124] [ActWorld-JEPA: Action-Conditioned JEPA World Models Meet Latent Planning for End-to-End Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16579)
*Ziying Song,Shengkai Zhang,Caiyan Jia,Lei Yang,Lin Liu,Yuchen Liu,Ziyang Liu,Zirui Li,Gang Wang,Peiliang Wu,Li Wang,Yadan Luo,Chen Lv*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 ActWorld-JEPA，将 JEPA 潜空间预测与候选轨迹选择连接：PC-JEPA 用全局与逐步轨迹意图引导循环潜预测并经门控交叉注意力融合；R³ 在规划监督下建模计划-未来表征一致性并作有界门控残差重排序。NAVSIM v1 navtest 94.1 PDMS，v2 navtest/navhard 91.6/42.7 EPDMS，nuScenes 1–6s 平均 L2 1.20 m、碰撞率 0.76%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: JEPA 能在潜空间做未来预测而无需像素重建，但"潜预测一致性"本身不足以刻画候选规划的相对驾驶质量，缺少从预测表征到候选选择的判别性桥梁。

Method: 两段式联合嵌入架构：(1) Plan-Conditioned JEPA——以全局与逐步轨迹意图条件化循环潜预测，再通过门控交叉注意力将预测未来与当前场景融合；(2) Relational Residual Reranking (R³)——在规划监督下建模计划表征与未来表征之间的一致/差异，对基础规划分数施加有界、门控的残差修正。推理时仅依赖观测上下文与候选轨迹。

Result: NAVSIM v1 navtest 94.1 PDMS（最终选择器用 navtest 聚合反馈校准，benchmark-specific 实现）；NAVSIM v2 navtest 91.6 EPDMS、navhard 42.7 EPDMS；nuScenes 1–6 s 平均 L2 误差 1.20 m、预测碰撞率 0.76%。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了从 JEPA 预测表征到由预期后果驱动驾驶决策的有效路径，表明潜空间预测可经关系型重排序转化为候选规划的相对质量判据。

Abstract: Joint-Embedding Predictive Architectures (JEPAs) enable future prediction in latent space without pixel reconstruction. However, latent predictive consistency alone does not establish the relative driving quality of candidate plans. We present ActWorld-JEPA, a joint-embedding formulation for candidate-based driving. Where Plan-Conditioned JEPA (PC-JEPA) uses global and step-wise trajectory intentions to guide recurrent latent prediction, then combines the predicted future with the current scene through gated cross-attention; Relational Residual Reranking ($R^3$) models agreement and discrepancy between plan and future representations under planning supervision, yielding bounded, gated residual corrections to the base planning scores. This formulation connects latent prediction to candidate selection using only observed context and proposed trajectories at inference. Benchmark-specific implementations report 94.1 PDMS on NAVSIM v1 navtest, with the final selector calibrated using aggregate NAVTEST feedback, and 91.6 and 42.7 EPDMS on NAVSIM v2 navtest and navhard, respectively. The nuScenes evaluation reports a mean $L_2$ error of 1.20 m and a predicted collision rate of 0.76% over 1--6 s. This work provides an effective path from JEPA's predictive representations to driving decisions informed by anticipated consequences.

</details>


### [125] [Do the Details Help the Whole? One-Way Transfer from Person Actions to Group Intent in VLMs on JRDB-Social](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16581)
*Chengguang Gan,Zhixi Cai,Yunhao Liang,Hanjun Wei,QingHao Zhang,Shiwen Ni*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文检验"同时学习个体动作(细节)与群体意图(整体)可相互促进"这一假设,结论是:动作→意图的方向性收益存在且稳健,而意图→动作无收益,且意图收益依赖正确、图像接地的动作描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社会场景数据集同时标注两个层级:每个人在做什么(个体动作/细节)与群体为何聚集(群体意图/整体)。文献与实践中常默认"两级联合训练对两者都有利",但该假设在视觉语言模型(VLM)上缺乏严格控制下的实证检验,作者因此以JRDB-Social为试验床系统验证。

Method: 以Qwen3.5 4B为主模型,在JRDB-Social上微调,设计八个训练臂×三个随机种子;核心臂在Qwen3.5 9B与Qwen3-VL 4B上复现;测试前固定判定规则(verdict rule)以避免选择性报告。辅以强制前缀(forced-prefix)测试、遮挡、成员掩码、线性探针与组件嫁接(component grafting),并做动作质量消融(空动作列表、借用同意图其他群体的动作)及"动作作为输入而非监督目标"的对照。

Result: 先写各成员动作再写意图,意图macro-F1由30.1升至31.0;先写意图则达31.2;动作F1与仅动作模型相差不超过0.2点。在选定配方下,任何含意图训练的臂都未提升动作性能。收益需正确且图像接地的动作:空列表或借用同意图他组动作会降低意图F1。探针与嫁接显示意图表征跟随已写出的动作、更多依赖人物区域、且部分驻留于视觉塔;将动作作为输入而非目标会同时损害两个层级。

Conclusion: "细节—整体"联合训练并非对称互惠:存在动作→意图的单向增益与可忽略的意图→动作效应,且意图增益以高质量动作表示为前提。作者借此提出以"先动作后意图"的生成顺序作为VLM社会场景推理的有效配方,并提示多层级标注的联合训练需谨慎假设其协同性。

Abstract: Social scene datasets label people at two levels: what each person is doing and what the group is there for. Training on both levels at once is often assumed to help each of them. We test this assumption for vision-language models (VLMs) on JRDB-Social, treating person actions as the details and group intent as the whole. We fine-tune Qwen3.5 4B under eight training arms with three seeds, repeat the core arms on Qwen3.5 9B and Qwen3-VL 4B, and judge every arm with a verdict rule fixed before testing. Writing each member's actions before the intent raises intent macro-F1 from 30.1 to 31.0, and writing the intent first reaches 31.2, while action F1 stays within 0.2 points of an action-only model. Under the selected recipe, no arm trained with intent improves actions. The gain requires correct, image-grounded actions: empty action lists or actions borrowed from another group with the same intent lower intent F1. Forced-prefix tests, occlusion, member masking, linear probes and component grafting show that the intent follows the written actions, relies more on people and partly resides in the vision tower. Supplying actions as input instead of as targets hurts both levels.

</details>


### [126] [ReFineR: Single image to dense point cloud registration at pixel level with point splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16583)
*Ugo Insalaco,Jean-Emmanuel Deschaud,Pierre Dellenbach*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ReFineR：基于可微分点泼溅渲染与平滑梯度方向测度的离线框架，将单幅图像精细配准到预重建点云，可自动估计点云尺度因子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相机与LiDAR观测互补，但实际机器人平台上即使完成标定，仍因时间不同步、机械振动或点云重建误差而存在数像素的残余重投影误差，在中距离可造成分米级几何偏差，影响稠密跨模态监督、纹理映射与语义标签迁移等下游任务。因此需要从单张图像与粗估计位姿出发进行精细配准。

Method: 提出离线框架ReFineR：以可微分点泼溅（point splatting）光栅化渲染为基础，结合平滑的梯度方向测度（Gradient Orientation Measure, GOM）作为相似性度量，通过“分析-合成”（analysis-by-synthesis）方式迭代优化相机位姿。此外可在优化前自动估计点云重缩放因子，使单一尺度不变的形式适配多种场景。

Result: 在由异构数据源采集并人工标定的定制数据集上进行评测；实验表明自动估计的点云尺度因子使方法在广泛场景下实现稳健的精细配准。

Conclusion: ReFineR能够有效消除残余重投影误差，实现单图像与点云的高精度精细配准，并具跨场景鲁棒性。

Abstract: Cameras and LiDARs (Light Detection and Ranging) provide complementary observations of the same scene: cameras capture rich appearance cues while LiDAR sensors provide accurate geometric measurements. In this work, we address the problem of fine image-to-point cloud registration from a single image and a roughly estimated camera pose. On robotic platforms, residual reprojection errors of several pixels are commonly observed even after calibration due to temporal desynchronization, mechanical vibrations, or inaccuracies in point cloud reconstruction. Such errors can lead to decimeter-level geometric discrepancies at moderate distances and significantly affect multiple downstream tasks, such as dense cross-modal supervision, texture mapping, and semantic label transfer. To address this challenge, we introduce ReFineR: an offline framework to finely register individual images to pre-reconstructed point clouds. ReFineR builds upon differentiable point splatting rasterization and a smooth Gradient Orientation Measure (GOM) to iteratively refine the camera pose through analysis-by-synthesis optimization. We evaluated the method on a curated dataset of manually calibrated images collected from heterogeneous data sources. Furthermore, we show that an automatic point cloud rescale factor can be estimated prior to optimization, enabling robust refinement across a wide range of scenarios using a single scale-invariant formulation. The project page can be found at https://refiner-project.github.io/

</details>


### [127] [Towards Democratising Low-Data Medical Image Classification: CPU-Trainable ZACH-ViT and Its Geometric Variants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16608)
*Athanasios Angelakis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文在严格资源约束下系统评测了四种超轻量ZACH-ViT变体与两种紧凑MIL基线（<260K参数、从零训练），跨7个MedMNIST+与3个外部基准，结果表明性能高度依赖任务与数据机制：Compact-TransMIL在23轮与全数据下均值最优，ZACH-Linear在47轮低数据下略优；CPU-only训练与4–9 ms单图推理可行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 资源受限场景下的医学图像分类需同时应对标注稀缺与算力不足，但现有紧凑模型缺乏在统一低参数、低数据条件下跨多数据集的系统对比与可复现的CPU可行性证据。

Method: 对比ZACH-ViT的linear、Euclidean prototype、Poincaré与spherical四种头，以及两个紧凑MIL基线；全部从零训练且参数量<260,000。实验覆盖7个MedMNIST+数据集与PCam、ISIC2017、深圳结核胸片三个外部基准；低数据协议为每类50张训练图、5个随机种子、按验证集选择23与47轮检查点，全数据参考仅用1个种子；另在ISIC2017与深圳数据上用4个CPU线程完成40次纯CPU训练。

Result: Compact-TransMIL在23轮与全数据下取得十数据集平均测试Macro-F1最高（53.34%、73.93%）；47轮时ZACH-Linear以57.76%对57.54%微弱领先。延长训练在300组配对比较中239组提升Macro-F1。ISIC2017上，低数据47轮以hZACH-Spherical最佳（44.09%，SD 4.09%），全数据以ZACH-EuclideanProto最佳（51.27%）。单图纯模型推理约4–9 ms，但图像解码与预处理显著抬高端到端时延。

Conclusion: 紧凑医学影像模型在严格资源约束下具备可行性（含纯CPU训练），但其优势具有明显的任务与训练机制依赖性，不能单一模型通吃；端到端部署瓶颈更多来自预处理而非模型推理。

Abstract: Medical image classification in resource-constrained settings must contend with limited annotations and computational capacity. We evaluate four ZACH-ViT variants with linear, Euclidean prototype, Poincare and spherical heads alongside two compact multiple-instance-learning baselines. All models are trained from scratch with fewer than 260,000 parameters across seven MedMNIST+ datasets and three external benchmarks (PCam, ISIC2017 and Shenzhen tuberculosis radiographs). The low-data protocol uses 50 training images per class and five seeds, with validation-selected checkpoints at 23 and 47 epochs; a full-data reference uses one seed. Compact-TransMIL achieves the highest ten-dataset mean test Macro-F1 at 23 epochs (53.34%) and with full data (73.93%). At 47 epochs, ZACH-Linear narrowly leads (57.76% versus 57.54% for Compact-TransMIL). Extending training improves Macro-F1 in 239 of 300 paired comparisons. On ISIC2017, hZACH-Spherical has the highest 47-epoch low-data Macro-F1 (44.09% with SD 4.09%), whereas ZACH-EuclideanProto leads with full data (51.27%). We additionally conduct 40 CPU-only training runs on ISIC2017 and Shenzhen using four CPU threads; model-only single-image inference requires approximately 4-9 ms, while image decoding and preprocessing substantially increase end-to-end latency. The results demonstrate the feasibility of CPU-only training on the tested platform and the task- and regime-dependence of compact medical imaging under strict resource constraints.

</details>


### [128] [Improved CNN-Transformer Network for Neutron Image Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16620)
*Yuheng Chu,Hongli Li,Tian Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种结合CNN与Transformer的超分辨率模型，通过微调预训练修复模型构建高质量参考图像，并将HPCF、MS-SEBlock与PCFN引入改进的CRAFT网络，显著提升中子图像的分辨率与细节保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中子图像空间分辨率对缺陷诊断、结构表征与无损检测至关重要，但受中子束质量、闪烁体转换效率与光散射、探测器像元尺寸及噪声水平限制，常规中子图像存在散斑噪声、边缘模糊和细节缺失，难以满足高精度分析需求；同时中子成像缺乏高质量清晰训练图像。

Method: 1）用真实中子图像微调预训练图像修复模型，用以增强现有中子图像，从而获得高质量参考图像用于超分辨率训练；2）在CRAFT框架基础上构建改进超分辨率网络，引入混合先验引导通道融合模块（HPCF）、均值-标准差双统计挤压激励块（MS-SEBlock）和部分卷积极前馈网络（PCFN），增强纹理与精细细节的重建能力。

Result: 仿真结果表明，与双三次插值、SRCNN、SRGAN-VGG等方法相比，所提网络在全参考指标PSNR、SSIM及无参考指标AGM、SMD上均取得更优表现，重建图像的视觉质量与结构保真度显著提升。

Conclusion: 该研究为中子图像质量提升提供了一条新途径。

Abstract: The spatial resolution of neutron images is of great importance for material defect diagnosis, structural characterization, and nondestructive testing. However, owing to limitations in neutron beam quality, scintillator conversion efficiency and light scattering, detector pixel size, and noise levels, conventional neutron images often suffer from speckle noise, blurred edges, and missing details, making them inadequate for high-precision analysis. To address these issues, an improved super-resolution model combining convolutional neural networks (CNNs) and Transformers is proposed. First, to overcome the difficulty of obtaining high-quality and clear training images for neutron imaging, a pretrained image restoration model is fine-tuned using real neutron images and then employed to enhance existing neutron images, thereby providing high-quality reference images for subsequent super-resolution model training. Second, an improved CRAFT-based super-resolution network is developed by incorporating a hybrid prior-guided channel fusion module (HPCF), a mean-standard deviation dual-statistics squeeze-and-excitation block (MS-SEBlock), and a partial convolution feed-forward network (PCFN), with the aim of improving the reconstruction of important information, such as textures and fine details, in neutron images. Simulation results demonstrate that, compared with bicubic interpolation, SRCNN, SRGAN-VGG, and other methods, the proposed network achieves superior performance in both full-reference metrics, including PSNR and SSIM, and no-reference metrics, including AGM and SMD. The visual quality and structural fidelity of the reconstructed images are also significantly improved. This study provides a new approach to improving neutron image quality.

</details>


### [129] [When Chekhov's Gun Fires Anyway: Over-Realization in Text-to-Image Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16630)
*Jiahao Huang,Kon Woo Kim,Song-Duo Ma,Yuka Kitamura,Felix Wagner,Yunshan Huang,Sunisth Kumar,Xanh Ho,Yun-Ang Wu,Zhijie Chen,Yunjia Xu,Junfeng Jiang,Zefan Yu,Fei Cheng,Akiko Aizawa*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出并度量文生图中的“过度实现”（over-realization）失败：提示词提及但未要求出现的目标仍被生成；构建 OverReal 基准评测八个系统，发现该失败普遍存在且被 CLIPScore 等常规指标奖励而非惩罚。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文生图模型常将提示中提及却未被要求呈现的目标实现在图像中（如否定、角色信念/想象、比喻喻体、视角遮挡之下），而先前研究仅深入探讨否定情形，且现有对齐与偏好指标不惩罚此类失败，缺乏系统度量手段。

Method: 基于心理空间理论界定四种触发情境；构建 OverReal-Gen（698 条覆盖四类情境的提示词，用于测量各生成器的过度实现率）与 OverReal-Det（2,841 张人工标注的生成图像，用于验证自动评判器）；评测八个文生图系统，并分析提示扩展的效果及扩散主干与线索词的注意力分配。

Result: 八个系统全部出现过度实现；CLIPScore 等常规指标反而奖励该失败；提示扩展仅部分缓解；生成器内部扩散主干对“被提及目标”的注意力几乎等同于“被要求目标”，且应抑制目标的线索词获得的注意力低于普通词。

Conclusion: 过度实现是文生图的普遍失败模式，现有指标存在方向性错误；需引入情境感知的评测基准与指标，并从注意力机制层面理解与改进模型对线索词的利用。

Abstract: In text-to-image generation, a target that the prompt mentions often appears in the generated image in a way the prompt does not call for. We call this failure over-realization. Mental-spaces theory points to the situations where it can happen: under a negation, inside what a character believes or imagines, as the vehicle of a figurative comparison, or behind a viewpoint that hides the target. Prior work has studied only negation in depth, and the conventional alignment and preference metrics do not penalize the failure. We build OverReal to measure it: OverReal-Gen, 698 prompts covering the four situations, on which we measure each generator's over-realization rate; and OverReal-Det, 2,841 generated images human-labeled for whether and how the target is over-realized, on which we verify the automatic judge that produces this rate. Our evaluation of eight text-to-image systems reveals that all of them over-realize, and that conventional metrics such as CLIPScore reward the failure rather than penalize it. Prompt expansion only partly mitigates the failure. Inside the generator, the diffusion backbone attends to a mentioned target almost as much as to a requested one. The cue words that should hold the target back receive less attention than ordinary words.

</details>


### [130] [Learning the Missing Derivative: Neural Multiple-Scattering Operators for Inverse Volume Rendering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16650)
*Sipeng Chen,Shibo Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 用神经算子预测多次散射场，并在反演渲染中对其求导，结合反演轨迹上的微调提升光学厚介质重建与重打光质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从照片重建光学厚介质需对多次散射及其对消光系数变化的导数建模；冻结光源断层成像丢弃该导数，可微路径追踪估计导数代价高且噪声大。此外，在留出介质上预测准确的算子，在优化器访问的密度状态下仍可能给出较差导数。

Method: 以神经算子预测多次散射场并在逆渲染中对其实施可微反传；透射率与单次散射直接解析计算。针对导数失配，使用蒙特卡洛散射场监督，在由该算子驱动的反演过程中产生的状态上做微调。

Result: 在留出的光学厚云场景上，等时间预算下重打光优于冻结光源断层成像；在显著更多时间预算下优于可微路径追踪。反演轨迹训练提升了访问状态处的导数对齐度，并减小优化漂移。

Conclusion: 神经算子结合反演轨迹微调可高效获得可用导数，实现高质量光学厚介质重建与重打光，兼顾精度与计算效率。

Abstract: Reconstructing optically thick media from photographs requires modeling multiple scattering and its response to changes in extinction. Frozen-source tomography discards this derivative; differentiable path tracing estimates it through expensive, noisy sampling. We instead predict the multiple-scattering field with a neural operator and differentiate through it during inverse rendering, while computing transmittance and single scattering directly. A further challenge arises during optimization: an operator that predicts the field accurately on held-out media can still produce poor derivatives at the density states visited by the optimizer. We address this by fine-tuning on states generated during inversions driven by the operator, using Monte Carlo scattering-field supervision. On held-out optically thick clouds, our method improves relighting over frozen-source tomography at equal time and outperforms differentiable path tracing given substantially more time. Training on inversion trajectories also improves derivative alignment at visited states and reduces optimization drift.

</details>


### [131] [MuViRL: Multimodal View-Invariant Visual Speech Representation Learning via Calibrated View Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16655)
*Fei Su,Kai Qiu,Juan Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MuViRL自监督框架，通过标定视角聚合与3D高斯视角合成实现跨视角一致的多模态视觉语音表征学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态场景的多模态表征需在跨视角一致的同时保留时序状态差异。视觉语音中视角变化会改变发音线索的可见性与可靠性，使得仅凭同步音视频难以学到视角不变表征。

Method: 将同步3D高斯视角合成与多模态自监督结合，从真实与合成数据学习一致表征；用多模态决策模型从教师预测损失中学习相对视角效用，并以标定分数指导互补信息聚合；引入Aggregation-to-Single蒸馏将聚合预测迁移至单分支；采用真实-合成对齐在跨视角匹配同步窗口并保持时序关系。

Result: 在真实与合成多视角数据上的实验表明，MuViRL提升了多模态表征一致性以及跨视角的视觉语音识别性能。

Conclusion: 标定视角聚合结合合成视角数据与蒸馏、时序对齐策略，可有效缓解视角变化带来的线索不可靠问题，获得视角不变的视觉语音表征。

Abstract: Multimodal representation learning combines complementary signals to capture shared semantic information. For dynamic scenes, these representations need to remain consistent across views while preserving distinctions between temporal states. In visual speech representation learning, view variation changes the visibility and reliability of articulation cues, making it difficult to learn such representations from synchronized audio and video. To address this challenge, we propose MuViRL, a self-supervised framework for multimodal view-invariant visual speech representation learning via calibrated view aggregation. The framework combines synchronized 3D Gaussian view synthesis with multimodal self-supervision to learn consistent representations from real and synthesized data. A multimodal decision model learns relative view utility from teacher prediction losses, and its calibrated scores guide the aggregation of complementary information. Aggregation-to-Single Distillation transfers the aggregated predictions to a single branch. Real-synthetic alignment matches synchronized windows across views while preserving their temporal relations. Experiments on real and synthesized multiview data demonstrate that MuViRL improves multimodal representation consistency and visual speech recognition performance across views.

</details>


### [132] [Sprint3D: Accelerating End-to-End 3D Asset Generation through Pipeline Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16701)
*Sho Kuno,Ruihan Yu,Yonghao Yu,Guixu Lin,Lian Fu,Muyao Niu,Yu-Ju Tsai,Zheng-Hui Huang,Yi-Chuan Huang,Siqi Yang,Fengbo Lan,Erwin Wu,Kaipeng Zhang,Zhixiang Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 从公开的图像到3D生成流水线Pixal3D出发，通过GPU化网格后处理与整流流蒸馏，将单资产中位耗时从133 s降至4.05 s（33×），并在法线指标上有所提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像到3D生成模型可产出资产级带纹理网格，但每个资产耗时数分钟，迭代慢、部署成本高。已有加速工作多聚焦网络采样加速，或在优化网格处理时自建生成器；本文希望不改生成器、直接在公开流水线上挖掘端到端可节省的时间。

Method: 对Pixal3D做性能剖析：四条rectified flow仅占墙钟14%，网格后处理占85%，其中主要开销是CPU端UV展开。据此将网格后处理迁移至GPU并移出关键路径，同时削减流程每次评估的额外开销，并通过蒸馏把采样从76次网络评估降至8次。

Result: 在单块H200 GPU、相同导出设置下，Sprint3D将单资产中位时间从133 s降至4.05 s（33×）。在2,653个held-out Toys4K对象上，Pixal3D协议的每项输入视角法线指标均提升，但平均轮廓IoU略低、点云对齐分数更低、细节纹理较弱。重建的后处理单独使共用导出代码的TRELLIS.2加速6.7×，且评测指标无可测变化。

Conclusion: 端到端瓶颈主要在生成之后的网格后处理而非网络采样，GPU化后处理配合流蒸馏可带来数量级加速，且后处理优化对其他同源流水线具备可迁移性；代价是纹理细节与部分几何指标存在轻微退化。

Abstract: Image-to-3D generative models now produce asset-quality textured meshes, but at minutes per asset they remain slow to iterate with and costly to deploy. Prior work has mostly accelerated the sampling of the networks, and where it also optimizes the mesh processing, it trains its own generators. We instead start from a publicly released pipeline and ask how much of its end-to-end time can be saved. Profiling the public Pixal3D pipeline shows that its latency is dominated by what happens after the networks: the four rectified flows take 14% of the wall-clock time and mesh post-processing takes 85%, most of it in CPU-based UV unwrapping. We therefore first rebuild the mesh post-processing on the GPU and take it off the critical path, then cut the per-evaluation overhead of the flows and distill them from 76 to 8 network evaluations. The resulting pipeline, Sprint3D, reduces the median time per asset from 133 s to 4.05 s on one H200 GPU at the same export settings (33$\times$). At the same time, on 2,653 held-out Toys4K objects it improves every input-view normal metric of the Pixal3D protocol, with a slightly lower mean silhouette IoU, lower point-cloud alignment scores and less fine texture detail. The rebuilt post-processing alone also speeds up TRELLIS.2, which uses the same export code, by 6.7$\times$ without a detectable change in the evaluated metrics.

</details>


### [133] [What Limits Thermal Infrared Drone Detection? A Cross-Dataset Benchmark Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16724)
*Tamara R. Lenhard,Jan Kels,Tobias Koch*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 系统刻画热红外无人机数据集在采集与处理流程中的变异性并建立伪影分类体系，发现检测性能主要受训练数据构成而非YOLO代际或模型容量支配，跨数据集泛化存在显著不对称，最优配置漏检率仍超40%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 热红外成像使无人机检测减少对环境光照的依赖，可实现超越可见光的连续监视；但公开红外无人机数据集在相机系统、处理流程与标注规范上存在大量且常未被记录的差异，导致检测性能评估与跨域泛化分析难以可靠比较。

Method: 系统梳理现有红外数据集特征，构建反映采集与处理流程来源的伪影分类学（覆盖光学与传感器效应、色调映射与图像增强、记录相关伪影）；聚焦长波红外数据，考察训练数据构成与模型特性对基于YOLO的检测性能、泛化能力及条件相关失效模式的影响。

Result: 检测性能更多由训练数据构成决定，而非YOLO代际或模型容量；跨数据集泛化呈现明显不对称性；最佳配置仍漏检超40%的无人机，在极小目标、杂乱城市背景和热交叉情形下漏检率更高。

Conclusion: 现有红外无人机数据集存在显著短板，数据集层面的变异与偏置是制约检测与泛化的关键因素，亟需标准化采集、处理与标注流程并针对性补充困难场景数据。

Abstract: Thermal infrared (IR) imaging enables drone detection with reduced dependence on ambient illumination, supporting continuous surveillance beyond the limitations of visible-spectrum sensing. However, substantial and often undocumented variability in camera systems, processing pipelines, and annotation practices across public IR drone datasets complicates the assessment of detector performance and cross-domain generalization. To examine these sources of variability and their implications for drone detection, we systematically characterize existing IR datasets and establish an artifact taxonomy reflecting their origins across the acquisition and processing pipeline, encompassing optical and sensor effects, tone mapping and image enhancement, and recording-related artifacts. Focusing on long-wave IR data, we investigate the impact of training data composition and model characteristics on YOLO-based detection performance, generalization, and condition-dependent failure modes. Our results demonstrate that detection performance is governed more strongly by training data composition than by YOLO generation or model capacity, while cross-dataset generalization exhibits pronounced asymmetries. Even the best-performing configuration fails to detect over 40\% of drones, with elevated miss rates for very small targets, cluttered urban backgrounds, and thermal crossover, highlighting the shortcomings of existing datasets.

</details>


### [134] [From Foundation VLM to Versatile Driving VLM: A Triplet Incremental Training Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16727)
*Shengtao Wu,Zichao Dong,Weixuan Qiao,Xiaolu Liu,Xin Zhan,Junbo Chen,Jianke Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Triplet Incremental Training框架，以非侵入方式将3D几何感知注入通用VLM，部署时丢弃深度分支实现零推理开销，在3D感知、驾驶推理、端到端规划与通用理解四类基准上超越SOTA驾驶VLM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将通用VLM适配自动驾驶通常被认为需要重型3D空间先验或侵入式架构改造以弥补空间grounding缺失；作者通过pilot study提出反直觉观点：现成通用VLM已具备跨任务适应性，真正缺失的关键环节是精确的metric depth，而这可通过车载传感器与深度基础模型轻易补足。

Method: Triplet Incremental Training：基于自动构建的（图像, 深度, QA对）三元组，采用统一三流训练流程——(1) 不冻结的主VLM流用于适配驾驶任务；(2) 辅助深度流，通过显式几何约束引导视觉backbone隐式想象3D场景几何；(3) 冻结的孪生VLM流，用logit级KL蒸馏防止语义漂移。部署时丢弃辅助深度分支，保证零推理开销。

Result: 在覆盖3D空间感知、驾驶导向语言推理、端到端规划、通用多模态理解四大能力的四个主要基准上评估：优于SOTA驾驶VLM，在3D感知上媲美领域专用专家模型，并成功保留原有通用推理能力。

Conclusion: Triplet Incremental Training提供了一种统一、可直接部署的范式，可将通用基础模型转化为稳健的驾驶智能，且无需架构改动与额外推理成本。
